(()=>{"use strict";
function runtimeExecutionProvenance(
  locationOrigin,
  binding,
  source,
  bytes,
  sha256,
  payloadBytes,
) {
  const result = (allowed, code, cargo = false, local = false, expectedFile = "") =>
    Object.freeze({ allowed, code, cargo, local, expectedFile });
  if (binding !== "cargo" && binding !== "local") {
    return result(false, "AFS_RUNTIME_PROVENANCE_BINDING_UNKNOWN");
  }
  const byteText = String(bytes ?? "");
  const byteCount = /^[1-9]\d*$/.test(byteText) ? Number(byteText) : 0;
  const hash = String(sha256 || "");
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(byteCount) || byteCount !== payloadBytes || !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(hash)) {
    return result(false, "AFS_RUNTIME_PROVENANCE_IDENTITY_INVALID");
  }
  const expectedFile = `afs-site-runtime-v6.0.8-${hash.slice(0, 16)}.txt`;
  let origin;
  let url;
  try {
    origin = new URL(locationOrigin);
    url = new URL(source);
  } catch {
    return result(false, "AFS_RUNTIME_PROVENANCE_SOURCE_INVALID");
  }
  if (origin.origin !== locationOrigin || url.href !== source || url.username || url.password
    || url.search || url.hash) {
    return result(false, "AFS_RUNTIME_PROVENANCE_SOURCE_INVALID");
  }
  if (binding === "cargo") {
    const parts = url.pathname.split("/");
    const valid = url.protocol === "https:"
      && url.hostname === "freight.cargo.site"
      && !url.port
      && parts.length === 4
      && parts[0] === ""
      && parts[1] === "m"
      && /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(parts[2])
      && parts[3] === expectedFile;
    return result(valid, valid ? "" : "AFS_RUNTIME_PROVENANCE_SOURCE_INVALID",
      valid, false, expectedFile);
  }
  if (!/^(?:127\.0\.0\.1|localhost)$/.test(origin.hostname)) {
    return result(false, "AFS_RUNTIME_PROVENANCE_LOCAL_ORIGIN_INVALID", false, true,
      expectedFile);
  }
  const expected = new URL(`/__afs-perfect/runtime/${expectedFile}`, origin.origin);
  const valid = url.origin === origin.origin && url.href === expected.href;
  return result(valid, valid ? "" : "AFS_RUNTIME_PROVENANCE_SOURCE_INVALID",
    false, valid, expectedFile);
}
function runtimeProvenanceFailure(root, code) {
  root.dataset.afsRuntimeLoader = "error";
  root.dataset.afsRuntimeLoaderError = code;
  root.dataset.afsRuntimeResourceState = "error";
  root.dataset.afsRuntimeResourceError = code;
  for (const name of [
    "data-afs-runtime-resource-binding",
    "data-afs-runtime-resource-source",
    "data-afs-runtime-resource-bytes",
    "data-afs-runtime-resource-sha256",
  ]) root.removeAttribute(name);
}

class SearchBatchObserver{
 constructor(callback,options={}){
  this.callback=callback;
  this.options=options||{};
  this.root=this.options.root||null;
  this.rootMargin=this.options.rootMargin||"0%";
  this.thresholds=Array.isArray(this.options.threshold)?this.options.threshold:[this.options.threshold??0];
  this.manual=!document.documentElement.hasAttribute("data-mode")&&Boolean(this.root?.classList?.contains("search-results"));
  this.targets=new Set;
  this.native=this.manual?null:new NativeObserver(callback,options);
 }
 observe(target){
  if(!this.manual)return this.native.observe(target);
  this.targets.add(target);
  Object.defineProperty(target,"__afsSearchBatchObserver",{configurable:true,value:this});
  target.dataset.afsSearchBatch="manual";
 }
 unobserve(target){
  if(!this.manual)return this.native.unobserve(target);
  this.targets.delete(target);
  if(target.__afsSearchBatchObserver===this)delete target.__afsSearchBatchObserver;
  target.removeAttribute("data-afs-search-batch");
 }
 disconnect(){
  if(!this.manual)return this.native.disconnect();
  for(const target of this.targets){if(target.__afsSearchBatchObserver===this)delete target.__afsSearchBatchObserver;target.removeAttribute("data-afs-search-batch")}
  this.targets.clear();
 }
 takeRecords(){return this.manual?[]:this.native.takeRecords()}
 deliver(target){
  if(!this.manual||!this.targets.has(target))return;
  const rect=target.getBoundingClientRect(),rootBounds=this.root?.getBoundingClientRect()||null;
  this.callback([{time:performance.now(),target,isIntersecting:true,intersectionRatio:1,boundingClientRect:rect,intersectionRect:rect,rootBounds}],this);
 }
}

state.searchLexicon={records,frequency,words,buckets,corrections:new Map}}function searchEditDistance(a,b,limit){const m=a.length,n=b.length;if(Math.abs(m-n)>limit)return limit+1;let limit+1;before=previous,previous=current}return previous[n]}function searchCorrections(word){if(word.length<6||/\d/.test(word))return[];const function searchModalQuickViewActive(){return document.querySelector('#afs-quick-view:not([hidden]),.quick-view.quick-view-animated-in,[role="dialog"].quick-view:not([hidden])')}
function searchModalExempt(node){const search=state.search;if(!(node instanceof HTMLElement))return!0;if(node===search.root||"tools"===node.id||node.matches('[data-search-menu-portal="true"],[data-afs-search-modal-root="true"],#afs-quick-view,.quick-view'))return!0;return Boolean(node.querySelector?.('#afs-quick-view,.quick-view'))}
function restoreSearchModalSibling(entry){const node=entry?.node;if(!node?.isConnected)return;entry.hadInert?node.setAttribute("inert",entry.inertValue||""):node.removeAttribute("inert"),entry.hadAriaHidden?node.setAttribute("aria-hidden",entry.ariaHiddenValue??""):node.removeAttribute("aria-hidden")}
function syncSearchModalSiblings(){const search=state.search;if(!search.root||"true"!==search.root.dataset.open)return;const retained=[];for(const entry of search.modalSiblings||[])if(searchModalExempt(entry.node))restoreSearchModalSibling(entry);else{entry.node.hasAttribute("inert")||entry.node.setAttribute("inert",""),"true"===entry.node.getAttribute("aria-hidden")||entry.node.setAttribute("aria-hidden","true"),retained.push(entry)}search.modalSiblings=retained;for(const node of document.body.children){if(searchModalExempt(node)||search.modalSiblings.some(entry=>entry.node===node))continue;search.modalSiblings.push({node:node,hadInert:node.hasAttribute("inert"),inertValue:node.getAttribute("inert"),hadAriaHidden:node.hasAttribute("aria-hidden"),ariaHiddenValue:node.getAttribute("aria-hidden")}),node.setAttribute("inert",""),node.setAttribute("aria-hidden","true")}}
function activateSearchModal(){const search=state.search;if(!search.root)return;search.root.setAttribute("aria-modal","true"),search.modalSiblings||(search.modalSiblings=[]),syncSearchModalSiblings(),search.modalObserver||(search.modalObserver=new MutationObserver(()=>queueMicrotask(syncSearchModalSiblings)),search.modalObserver.observe(document.body,{childList:!0,subtree:!0,attributes:!0,attributeFilter:["inert","aria-hidden"]}))}
function deactivateSearchModal(){const search=state.search;search.modalObserver?.disconnect(),search.modalObserver=null;for(const entry of search.modalSiblings||[])restoreSearchModalSibling(entry);search.modalSiblings=[],search.redirectingFocus=!1}
function searchModalRoots(){const search=state.search;return[document.querySelector("#tools .modes"),search.root,...document.querySelectorAll('[data-search-menu-portal="true"],[data-afs-search-modal-root="true"]')].filter(root=>root?.isConnected&&!root.hidden&&"true"!==root.getAttribute("aria-hidden"))}
function searchModalContains(node){return node instanceof Element&&searchModalRoots().some(root=>root===node||root.contains(node))}
function searchModalFocusable(){const found=[];for(const root of searchModalRoots())for(const node of root.querySelectorAll(searchModalFocusableSelector)){if(!(node instanceof HTMLElement)||node.matches(".search-shade")||node.hidden||node.closest('[hidden],[inert],[aria-hidden="true"]'))continue;const style=getComputedStyle(node);if("none"===style.display||"hidden"===style.visibility||!node.getClientRects().length)continue;found.includes(node)||found.push(node)}return found.sort((a,b)=>a===b?0:a.compareDocumentPosition(b)&Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING?-1:1)}

").map(parseFloat),x=v[0]||0,y=isNaN(v[1])?x:v[1];return[Math.min(w/2,Math.max(0,x)),Math.min(h/2,Math.max(0,y))]},arc=(pts,cx,cy,rx,ry,a0)=>{for(let j=0;j<=16;j+=1){const w=R-L,h=B-T,pts=[],q=r=>Math.max(0,r-inset),[a,c]=rad(s,"borderTopRightRadius",w,h),[d,e]=rad(s,"borderBottomRightRadius",w,h),[g,k]=rad(s,"borderBottomLeftRadius",w,h),[m,n]=rad(s,"borderTopLeftRadius",w,h);arc(pts,R-a,T+c,q(a),q(c),-90);arc(pts,R-d,B-e,q(d),q(e),0);arc(pts,L+g,B-k,q(g),q(k),90);arc(pts,L+m,T+n,q(m),q(n),180);return pts},inward=(v,end)=>(end?Math.floor(v*dpr+.001):Math.ceil(v*dpr-.001))/dpr,L=inward(panelBox.left,0)-bounds.left,T=inward(panelBox.top,0)-bounds.top,R=inward(panelBox.right,1)-bounds.left,B=inward(panelBox.bottom,1)-bounds.top,out=loop(0,0,W,H,ms,ins).map(([x,y])=>fx(x)+" "+fy(y)),start=out[0];out.push(start);if(R>L&&B>T){const pp=loop(L,T,R,B,cs,0).map(([x,y])=>f(x)+" "+f(y));out.push(...pp,pp[0],start)}menu.style.setProperty("clip-path","polygon("+out.join(",")+")","important");const observer=state.search.afsCutObserver||(state.search.afsCutObserver=new ResizeObserver(()=>requestAnimationFrame(syncOpenSelect))),seen=state.search.afsCutObserved||(state.search.afsCutObserved=new WeakSet);for(const el of[box,menu])seen.has(el)||(seen.add(el),observer.observe(el))}function positionSelect(root){if(!root||"true"!==root.dataset.open)return;const afsOrigin=afsFixedOrigin(),triggerBox=trigger.getBoundingClientRect(),panelBox=box.getBoundingClientRect(),vmin=afsCssVmin(box),gutter=5*vmin,taxonomy=root===state.search.taxonomy,targetWidth=(taxonomy?70:45)*vmin,popoverWidth=Math.min(panelBox.width,Math.max(triggerBox.width,targetWidth)),popoverLeft=Math.min(Math.max(triggerBox.left-afsOrigin.left,gutter),innerWidth-gutter-popoverWidth),top=triggerBox.bottom-afsOrigin.top+.625*vmin;menu.dataset.searchMenuLayout="overlay",menu.style.setProperty("--search-popover-left",popoverLeft/vmin+"vmin"),menu.style.setProperty("--search-popover-width",popoverWidth/vmin+"vmin"),menu.style.setProperty("--search-popover-max-height","none"),menu.style.setProperty("--search-popover-top",top/vmin+"vmin");

setSearchResultState(kind,message="",total=0,loaded=0){const search=state.search;if(!search.root)return;search.root.dataset.state=kind,search.root.dataset.afsResultState=kind,search.root.dataset.afsResultTotal=String(total),search.root.dataset.afsResultLoaded=String(loaded),search.list&&(search.list.dataset.afsResultState=kind);if("loading"===kind)search.root.setAttribute("aria-busy","true");else function searchRenderedResultCount(){return[...state.search.list?.querySelectorAll(".search-group[data-result-group] > .search-group-results")||[]].reduce((total,panel)=>total+Number(panel.dataset.rendered||0),0)}
function searchDisplayedResultCount(){return state.search.list?.querySelectorAll(".search-group-results > .search-item").length||0}
function syncSearchResultProgress(){const search=state.search,resultTotal=search.results.length,logicalTotal=search.results.uniqueTotal??resultTotal,loaded=Math.min(searchRenderedResultCount(),resultTotal),complete=loaded>=resultTotal,shown=Math.min(searchDisplayedResultCount(),logicalTotal),message=complete?logicalTotal+" result"+(1===logicalTotal?"":"s"):logicalTotal+" result"+(1===logicalTotal?"":"s")+" - "+shown+" shown";setSearchResultState(complete?"complete":"partial",message,logicalTotal,shown)}
function renderSearch(){const search=state.search;if(!search.root||"true"!==search.root.dataset.open)return;search.observer?.disconnect(),search.observer=null;const query=search.input.value,searchProfileStarted=performance.now(),active=Boolean(query.trim()||search.section||search.category||search.filetype||search.filter||search.year);search.root.dataset.typed=String(Boolean(query.trim())),releaseCardCaptionObservers(search.list),search.list.replaceChildren(),search.list.scrollTop=0,search.results=[],search.rendered=0,search.groupSerial=0;if("loading"===state.dataStatus)return void setSearchResultState("loading","Loading search results…");if("error"===state.dataStatus)return void setSearchResultState("error","Search results unavailable");if(!active)return void setSearchResultState("empty","Search by keyword or choose a filter");const searchFindStarted=performance.now(),matchedResults=find(query),searchFindEnded=performance.now(),results=prepareResults(matchedResults,query),searchPrepareEnded=performance.now(),total=results.uniqueTotal??results.length;search.results=results;if(!total)return void setSearchResultState("empty","No results");const searchAppendStarted=performance.now();appendSearchBatch();if(new URL(location.href).searchParams.has("afs-profile")){const searchProfileEnded=performance.now();search.root.dataset.afsSearchProfile=JSON.stringify({query,findMs:searchFindEnded-searchFindStarted,prepareMs:searchPrepareEnded-searchFindEnded,appendMs:searchProfileEnded-searchAppendStarted,totalMs:searchProfileEnded-searchProfileStarted,matched:matchedResults.length,prepared:results.length})}}function taxonomyResultKey(result){return"works"===result.group?`work|${workIdentity(result.record)}`:"media"===result.record.kind?`media|${result.record.i}`:`record|${urlKey(result.record.u)}|${normalize(displayTitle(result.record))}|${result.record.kind}`}
function taxonomyCount(results){return new Set(results.map(taxonomyResultKey)).size}
function taxonomyBucket(group,section=""){return state.search.results.filter(result=>result.group===group&&(section?result.sectionGroup===section:!result.sectionGroup))}
function ensureSearchProgressObserver(){return null}

function appendGroupMore(panel,group,section=""){const previous=panel.querySelector(":scope > .search-sentinel");previous&&(state.search.observer?.unobserve?.(previous),previous.remove());const bucket=taxonomyBucket(group,section),rendered=Number(panel.dataset.rendered||0);if(rendered>=bucket.length)return;const sentinel=make("li","search-sentinel search-group-sentinel"),button=make("button","search-more","More"),remaining=bucket.length-rendered;button.type="button",button.dataset.afsControl="more",button.setAttribute("aria-label","Show 20 more search results ("+remaining+" remaining in "+(section?section+" / ":"")+group+")"),button.setAttribute("aria-pressed","false"),listen(button,"click",event=>{event.preventDefault(),event.stopPropagation(),loadSearchGroup(button,panel,group,section,20)}),sentinel.append(button),panel.append(sentinel)}

textMentionSnippet(record,query){query=vocabularyQuery(query);const text=String(record.h||"").replace(/\s+/g," container.childElementCount?container:null}function searchVisualSourceRecord(record){if(!record)return null;if("media"===record.kind)return record.th||originalMediaUrl(record)||/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(displayTitle(record))?record:null;const route=urlKey(record.u||"/"),hash=mediaHash(record),stableIds=unique([record.i,record.h,record.hash]).map(value=>plainText(value)).filter(Boolean),candidates=state.media.filter(candidate=>urlKey(candidate.u||"/")===route&&Boolean(candidate.th||originalMediaUrl(candidate)||/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(displayTitle(candidate))));if(hash){const null}function htmlRecordCitation(record,view=presentation(record)){const page=state.pages.find(item=>urlKey(item.u)===urlKey(record.u));let title=plainText(view.title||record.t||record.p||"").replace(/^(?:a\s*\+\s*o\s*[−-]|a\s+plus\s+o\s+minus|α\s*\+\s*ο\s*-)\s*[,;:–—-]?\s*/i,"").trim(),author="a plus o minus"===normalize(record.s)?"α + ο rememberAuthoredPppDecks(){
 const decks=state.archive.nativePppDecks||(state.archive.nativePppDecks=new Map);
 if(state.mode==="html")return decks;
 const afsPplusSlug=slug=>{
  if(String(slug||"").toLowerCase()==="pplus-n")return{internalClass:"ppplus-n",label:"P+ N"};
  const m=/^ppp?lus-(v|iv|iiii|iii|ii|i)$/.exec(String(slug||"").toLowerCase());
  if(!m)return null;
  const numeral=m[1]==="iv"?"iiii":m[1];
  return{internalClass:"ppplus-"+numeral,label:"P+ "+(m[1]==="iv"?"IV":numeral.toUpperCase())};
 };
 for(const page of document.querySelectorAll('.pages .page[page-url]')){
  const slug=page.getAttribute('page-url'),info=afsPplusSlug(slug);
  if(!info)continue;
  const list=page.querySelector('bodycopy .'+info.internalClass+' ol');
  if(!list||list.querySelector('[data-afs-html-resource],[data-afs-html-resource-source]'))continue;
  const cards=list.querySelectorAll(':scope > li').length;
  if(cards)decks.set('/'+slug,{route:'/'+slug,className:info.internalClass,label:info.label,cards,markup:list.outerHTML});
 }
 // Cargo's serialized native content survives a full HTML-mode document replacement.
 // Read it only when the live native list was removed by the visual-mode projection.
 let nativePages=state.archive.nativeCargoPages;
 if(nativePages===undefined){
  nativePages=globalThis.__PRELOADED_STATE__?.pages?.byId||null;
  if(!nativePages){
   const prefix="window.__PRELOADED_STATE__=";
   const source=[...document.scripts].find(script=>script.textContent.trim().startsWith(prefix));
   if(source)try{nativePages=JSON.parse(source.textContent.trim().slice(prefix.length).replace(/;$/," ")).pages?.byId||null}catch{}
  }
  state.archive.nativeCargoPages=nativePages;
 }
 for(const page of Object.values(nativePages||{})){
  const slug=String(page.purl||""),info=afsPplusSlug(slug);
  if(!info||decks.has("/"+slug)||typeof page.content!=="string")continue;
  const template=document.createElement("template");template.innerHTML=page.content;
  const list=template.content.querySelector("."+info.internalClass+" ol");
  if(!list||list.querySelector("[data-afs-html-resource],[data-afs-html-resource-source]"))continue;
  const cards=list.querySelectorAll(":scope > li").length;
  if(cards)decks.set("/"+slug,{route:"/"+slug,className:info.internalClass,label:info.label,cards,markup:list.outerHTML});
 }
 return decks;

cardGraphemes(value){const text=String(value||"").trim();return"Segmenter"in Intl?[...new Intl.Segmenter(void 0,{granularity:"grapheme"}).segment(text)].map(item=>item.segment):Array.from(text)}
function cardSeriesSize(record,stem){const list=state.archive?.records;if(!Array.isArray(list))return 0;const cache=state.cardSeriesCache||(state.cardSeriesCache=new Map),cacheKey=urlKey(record.url)+"|"+stem;if(!cache.has(cacheKey)){const numbers=new Set;for(const other of list){if(urlKey(other.url)!==urlKey(record.url))continue;const otherTitle=archiveTitle(other).replace(/^(?:A\+O[−-]|α\s*\+\s*ο\s*-|a\+o-)\s*,\s*/i,"").replace(/\s+[([][^()[\]]*[)\]]/g,"").trim();const otherStem=otherTitle.replace(/(?:,\s*|\s+[—–-]\s+|\s+)(?:Installation View\s+)?\d{1,5}\s*$/i,"").trim();if(otherStem===stem)numbers.add((otherTitle.match(/(\d{1,5})\s*$/)||["",""])[1])}cache.set(cacheKey,numbers.size)}return cache.get(cacheKey)}
function cardIdentifier(record){const authored=state.config?.cardCodes,routeKey=urlKey(record.url);if(authored){const byRoute=authored[routeKey];if(typeof byRoute==="string")return byRoute;if(byRoute&&typeof byRoute==="object"){const byTitle=byRoute[archiveTitle(record)];if(byTitle)return byTitle}}const pppCode={"/ppplus-i":"PILOT","/ppplus-ii":"MLK40","/ppplus-iii":"HUO25","/ppplus-iiii":"LASCAUX","/pplus-n":"LABEL","/pplus-i":"PILOT","/pplus-ii":"MLK40","/pplus-iii":"HUO25","/pplus-iv":"LASCAUX","/pplus-v":"GOLDEN"}[routeKey];if(pppCode)return pppCode;if(["p","ppp","p plus","ppplus","pplus"].includes(normalize(record.section))){const page=String(record.pageTitle||"");if(/(?:PPP|P\s*plus|P\+)\s*V\b|GOLDEN/i.test(page))return"GOLDEN";if(/(?:PPP|P\s*plus|P\+)\s*(?:IIII|IV)\b|LASCAUX/i.test(page))return"LASCAUX";if(/(?:PPP|P\s*plus|P\+)\s*III\b|HUO25/i.test(page))return"HUO25";if(/(?:PPP|P\s*plus|P\+)\s*II\b|MLK4\d/i.test(page))return"MLK40";if(/(?:PPP|P\s*plus|P\+)\s*N\b|NULLA/i.test(page))return"LABEL";if(/(?:PPP|P\s*plus|P\+)\s*I\b|PILOT/i.test(page))return"PILOT"}let title=archiveTitle(record);const chapter=title.match(/Hans Ulrich Obrist Archives?[^\d]*(?:Chapter|CH)\s*(\d+)/i);if(chapter)return"HUOA"+chapter[1];if(/^scandale? drama\s*<3$/i.test(title))return"SD<3";title=title.replace(/^(?:A\+O[−-]|α\s*\+\s*ο\s*-|a\+o-)\s*,\s*/i,"").replace(/\s+[([][^()[\]]*[)\]]/g,"").trim();const stem=title.replace(/(?:,\s*|\s+[—–-]\s+|\s+)(?:Installation View\s+)?\d{1,5}\s*$/i,"").trim();if(stem!==title&&(/,\s*\d{1,5}$|Installation View\s+\d+$/i.test(title)||cardSeriesSize(record,stem)>1))title=stem.replace(/,\s*Installation View$/i,"");title=title.replace(/\s+\d{4}(?:\s*[-–]\s*\d{4})?$/,"").replace(/(?<![A-Z]\.[A-Z]?)[\s,;:·—–-]+$/,"").trim();if(!/[\p{L}\p{N}]/u.test(title))return compactSectionLabel(record.section);const points=cardGraphemes(title);if(points.length<=10)return title;let words=title.split(/\s+/).map(word=>word.replace(/^[^\p{L}\p{N}#]+|[^\p{L}\p{N}]+$/gu,"").replace(/^(?:l|d|qu|j|n|s|c)[’'](?=\p{L})/iu,"")).filter(word=>/[\p{L}\p{N}]/u.test(word));if(words.length>3&&words.every(word=>cardGraphemes(word).length===1))words=[words.join("")];if(words.length===1){const word=words[0],graphemes=cardGraphemes(word);return graphemes.length<=10?word:graphemes.slice(0,7).join("")+"."}const stopWords=new Set(["a","an","and","for","in","of","on","the","to","le","la","les","un","une","des","de","du","et","au","aux","en","sur","pour","par","avec","dans","son","sa","ses","il","lo","gli","di","del","della","dei","e","con","per","una","der","die","das","und","von","zu"]);const content=words.filter(word=>!stopWords.has(normalize(word)));let code="";for(const word of content){const part=/^\d+$/.test(word)?word:/^#/.test(word)?"#":cardGraphemes(word)[0].toUpperCase();if(cardGraphemes(code+part).length>8)break;code+=part}if(cardGraphemes(code).length>=2)return code;const fallback=content[0]||words[0]||title,fallbackGraphemes=cardGraphemes(fallback);return fallbackGraphemes.length<=10?fallback:fallbackGraphemes.slice(0,7).join("")+"."}
function cardFooterLabel(record){const routeKey=urlKey(record.url);const pppDeck={"/ppplus-i":"I","/ppplus-ii":"II","/ppplus-iii":"III","/ppplus-iiii":"IV","/pplus-n":"N","/pplus-i":"I","/pplus-ii":"II","/pplus-iii":"III","/pplus-iv":"IV","/pplus-v":"V"}[routeKey];if(pppDeck)return pppDeck;if(["p","ppp","p plus","ppplus","pplus"].includes(normalize(record.section))){const page=String(record.pageTitle||"");return/(?:PPP|P\s*plus|P\+)\s*V\b|GOLDEN/i.test(page)?"V":/(?:PPP|P\s*plus|P\+)\s*(?:IIII|IV)\b|LASCAUX/i.test(page)?"IV":/(?:PPP|P\s*plus|P\+)\s*III\b|HUO25/i.test(page)?"III":/(?:PPP|P\s*plus|P\+)\s*II\b|MLK4\d/i.test(page)?"II":/(?:PPP|P\s*plus|P\+)\s*N\b|NULLA/i.test(page)?"N":"I"}return compactSectionLabel(record.section)}
function cardSoloShowArtist(record){if(!record||!("Exhibition Making"===record.section||record.categories?.includes("shows")))return"";const explicit=String(record.author||"").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();if(explicit)return explicit;const label=String(record.accessibleLabel||"").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim(),candidate=label.split(",",1)[0]?.trim()||"",words=candidate.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);if(!candidate||words.length<2||words.length>5||/^\d{4}$/.test(candidate)||normalize(candidate)===normalize(record.title)||normalize(candidate)===normalize(record.pageTitle))return"";const context=normalize([record.pageTitle,record.title,record.url].filter(Boolean).join(" ")),nameWords=normalize(candidate).split(/\s+/).filter(word=>word.length>1);return nameWords.length&&nameWords.every(word=>context.includes(word))?candidate:""}

function htmlInternalDestination(value){if(!value)return!1;try{const url=new URL(value,location.href);return url.origin===location.origin||url.hostname==="arthurfouray.systems"}catch{return String(value).startsWith("#")||String(value).startsWith("/")}}
function toRoman(num){const table=[[1000,"M"],[900,"CM"],[500,"D"],[400,"CD"],[100,"C"],[90,"XC"],[50,"L"],[40,"XL"],[10,"X"],[9,"IX"],[5,"V"],[4,"IV"],[1,"I"]];let n=Math.max(1,Math.floor(num)||1),out="";for(const[v,s]of table){while(n>=v){out+=s;n-=v;}}return out}globalThis.__afsToRoman=toRoman;
function htmlPageName(value,fallback=""){const raw=String(value||"").trim(),fragment=raw.startsWith("#")?raw.slice(1).split(/[?&]/,1)[0]:"";let decoded=fragment;try{decoded=decodeURIComponent(fragment)}catch{}const indexed=fragment?[...document.querySelectorAll(".afs-page-index-link")].find(link=>link.getAttribute("href")===raw)?.textContent.trim():"";if(indexed)return publicText(indexed);const path=urlKey(decoded?"/"+decoded:raw||"/"),aliases={"/":"Systems","/systems":"Systems","/ppplus":"P+","/ppplus-i":"P+ I","/ppplus-ii":"P+ II","/ppplus-iii":"P+ III","/ppplus-iiii":"P+ "+toRoman(4),"/pplus":"P+","/pplus-n":"P+ N","/pplus-i":"P+ I","/pplus-ii":"P+ II","/pplus-iii":"P+ III","/pplus-iv":"P+ "+toRoman(4),"/pplus-v":"P+ "+toRoman(5),"/before-arts":"before ARTS","/pre-natal":"Pre-Natal","/mmm":"mmm","/a-plus-o-minus":"A + O -","/arthur-fouray":"Arthur Fouray","/exhibition-making":"Exhibition Making","/screen":"SCREEN","/rt4a":"rt4a","/music":"Music"};if(aliases[path])return aliases[path];const record=(state.pages||[]).find(item=>urlKey(item.u||"")===path),title=record?String(record.t||record.p||"").trim():"";if(title)return publicText(title).replace(/\s*[—|-]\s*Arthur Fouray(?: Systems)?\s*$/i,"").trim();const supplied=plainText(fallback);if(!fragment&&supplied)return supplied;const slug=path.replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g,"").split("/").pop()||"Systems";return slug.replace(/[-_]+/g," ").replace(/\b\w/g,letter=>letter.toUpperCase())}
function htmlResourceKind(value,item=null){const source=String(value||""),mime=String(item?.getAttribute?.("type")||item?.getAttribute?.("mime-type")||item?.dataset?.mimeType||"").toLowerCase(),tag=String(item?.tagName||"").toLowerCase(),embedded=item?.querySelector?.("video,audio,img,picture"),element="media-item"===tag?String(embedded?.tagName||"").toLowerCase():tag;return"application/pdf"===mime||/\.pdf(?:$|[?#])/i.test(source)?"pdf":"video"===element||mime.startsWith("video/")||/\.(?:mp4|m4v|mov|webm)(?:$|[?#])/i.test(source)?"video":"audio"===element||mime.startsWith("audio/")||/\.(?:mp3|m4a|aac|wav|aif|aiff|ogg|flac)(?:$|[?#])/i.test(source)?"audio":["img","picture"].includes(element)||mime.startsWith("image/")||/\.(?:avif|bmp|gif|heic|heif|ico|jpe?g|png|svg|tiff?|webp)(?:$|[?#])/i.test(source)?"image":"html"}

function searchQuickViewDescriptor(item){const owner=item.closest?.("[data-afs-page-id][data-afs-media-id]")||item.querySelector?.("[data-afs-page-id][data-afs-media-id]")||item,record=searchQuickViewRecord(owner),view=record?presentation(record):null,pageId=owner?.dataset.afsPageId||searchQuickViewIds(record).pageId,mediaId=owner?.dataset.afsMediaId||searchQuickViewIds(record).mediaId,mediaUrl=owner?.dataset.afsMediaSource||(record?originalMediaUrl(record):"")||item.getAttribute?.("contenturl")||item.getAttribute?.("src")||item.querySelector?.('meta[itemprop="contentUrl"]')?.content||"",title=publicText(owner?.dataset.afsMediaTitle||view?.title||(record?displayTitle(record):"")||item.getAttribute?.("title")||item.getAttribute?.("alt")||filename(mediaUrl)||"Media"),caption=publicText(owner?.dataset.afsMediaCaption||record?.al||record&&bibliographicLabel(view?.author,view?.title,record.y,view?.description)||title);let parts=null;try{parts=record&&qvParts(record)}catch{}return{pageId,mediaId,mediaUrl,title,caption,artist:parts?publicText(parts.artist):"",work:parts?publicText(parts.title):"",meta:parts?publicText(parts.meta):""}}
function installNativeQuickViewCaption(descriptor){
 const search=state.search;
 search.nativeQuickViewCaptionObserver?.disconnect();clearTimeout(search.nativeQuickViewCaptionTimer);
 let watched=null;
 const stop=()=>{observer.disconnect();clearTimeout(search.nativeQuickViewCaptionTimer);if(search.nativeQuickViewCaptionObserver===observer)search.nativeQuickViewCaptionObserver=null;};
 const decorate=()=>{
  const view=document.querySelector(".quick-view.quick-view-open,.quick-view.quick-view-visible");
  if(!view){if(watched)stop();return;}
  if(view!==watched){watched=view;observer.disconnect();observer.observe(view,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:["class"]});if(view.parentNode)observer.observe(view.parentNode,{childList:true});clearTimeout(search.nativeQuickViewCaptionTimer);}
  const expectedHash=descriptor.mediaUrl?.match(/\/i\/([^/]+)/)?.[1];
  const current=[...view.querySelectorAll('.quick-view-item')].find(n=>{const r=n.getBoundingClientRect();return r.width>0&&r.left<=innerWidth/2&&r.right>=innerWidth/2;});
  const currentHash=current?.getAttribute('hash')||current?.getAttribute('src')?.match(/\/i\/([^/]+)/)?.[1];
  if(expectedHash&&currentHash&&expectedHash!==currentHash){stop();return;}
  const caption=view.querySelector("[data-quick-view-caption]");if(!caption)return;
  const artist=publicText(descriptor.artist||""),title=publicText(descriptor.work||descriptor.title||"Untitled"),full=publicText((descriptor.work?descriptor.meta:descriptor.caption)||""),description=normalize(full)!==normalize(title)?full:"",heading=artist?artist+", "+title:title;
  if(caption.dataset.afsPageId===descriptor.pageId&&caption.dataset.afsMediaId===descriptor.mediaId&&caption.querySelector(".afs-native-quick-view__title")?.textContent===heading&&(caption.querySelector(".afs-native-quick-view__description")?.textContent||"")===description&&!caption.classList.contains("hidden"))return;
  const titleNode=qvTitleNode("afs-native-quick-view",artist,title),descriptionNode=description?make("span","afs-native-quick-view__description",description):null;
  caption.replaceChildren(...(descriptionNode?[titleNode,descriptionNode]:[titleNode]));caption.classList.remove("hidden");caption.dataset.afsCaptionReady="true";caption.dataset.afsPageId=descriptor.pageId;caption.dataset.afsMediaId=descriptor.mediaId;
 };
 const observer=new MutationObserver(decorate);observer.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:["class"]});search.nativeQuickViewCaptionObserver=observer;
 search.nativeQuickViewCaptionTimer=setTimeout(stop,30000);queueMicrotask(decorate);
}

function visualInflowMode(){return["img","card"].includes(state.mode)}

function visualInflowOptions(){
  const search=state.search,query=String(search.input?.value||"").trim(),categories=selectedValues(search.category),types=selectedValues(search.filetype),filters=selectedValues(search.filter),years=selectedValues(search.year);
  return{query:query,categories:categories,types:types,filters:filters,years:years,active:Boolean(query||categories.length||types.length||filters.length||years.length),key:[query,selectionValue(categories),selectionValue(types),selectionValue(filters),selectionValue(years)].join("\u001f")}
}
function visualInflowRecordIdentity(record){return String(record?.i||record?.eid||record?.id||"")}
function visualInflowRecordMaps(){
  const cached=state.search.visualRecordMaps;
  if(cached&&cached.count===state.media.length)return cached;
  const byId=new Map,byHash=new Map;
  for(const record of state.media){const id=visualInflowRecordIdentity(record),hash=mediaHash(record);id&&byId.set(id,record),hash&&(byHash.get(hash)||byHash.set(hash,[]).get(hash)).push(record)}
  return state.search.visualRecordMaps={count:state.media.length,byId:byId,byHash:byHash}
}
function visualInflowSourceRecords(record){
  const maps=visualInflowRecordMaps(),found=new Set,id=String(record?.id||record?.i||""),hash=String(record?.hash||"");
  id&&maps.byId.has(id)&&found.add(maps.byId.get(id));
  for(const source of maps.byHash.get(hash)||[])found.add(source);
  if(!found.size){const route=urlKey(record?.url||"/"),sourceUrl=String(record?.originalUrl||record?.mediaUrl||"");for(const source of state.media)urlKey(source.u)===route&&(sourceUrl&&[source.q,source.th].includes(sourceUrl)||visualInflowRecordIdentity(source)===id)&&found.add(source)}
  return[...found]
}
function visualInflowFacetIndex(results,allRoutes=!1){
  const ids=new Set,hashes=new Set,entityRoutes=new Set,routes=new Set;
  for(const result of results||[]){const record=result.record||result,id=visualInflowRecordIdentity(record),hash=mediaHash(record),route=urlKey(record.u||"/");id&&ids.add(id),hash&&hashes.add(hash),routes.add(route),!searchFileType(record)&&entityRoutes.add(route)}
  return{ids:ids,hashes:hashes,entityRoutes:entityRoutes,routes:allRoutes?routes:entityRoutes}
}
function visualInflowFilterState(options=visualInflowOptions()){
  const cached=state.search.visualFilterState;
  if(cached?.key===options.key)return cached;
  const blank={section:"",category:"",filetype:"",filter:"",year:""},query=options.query?visualInflowFacetIndex(find(options.query,blank),!0):null,categories=options.categories.length?visualInflowFacetIndex(find("",{...blank,category:selectionValue(options.categories)})):null,filters=options.filters.length?visualInflowFacetIndex(find("",{...blank,filter:selectionValue(options.filters)})):null,years=options.years.length?visualInflowFacetIndex(find("",{...blank,year:selectionValue(options.years)})):null;
  return state.search.visualFilterState={key:options.key,options:options,query:query,categories:categories,filters:filters,years:years}
}
function visualInflowFacetMatches(record,rawRecords,index,useAllRoutes=!1){
  if(!index)return!0;
  const route=urlKey(record?.url||rawRecords[0]?.u||"/");
  if((useAllRoutes?index.routes:index.entityRoutes).has(route))return!0;
  return rawRecords.some(raw=>index.ids.has(visualInflowRecordIdentity(raw))||index.hashes.has(mediaHash(raw)))
}
function visualInflowTextMatches(record,query){
  const phrase=normalize(query),text=normalize([record?.title,record?.pageTitle,record?.description,record?.author,record?.accessibleLabel,record?.section,record?.url].filter(Boolean).join(" "));
  if(!phrase)return!0;
  if(fieldPhraseMatch(text,phrase))return!0;
  const words=phrase.split(" ").filter(word=>word.length>1&&!['the','of','and'].includes(word));
  return Boolean(words.length)&&words.every(word=>fieldPhraseMatch(text,word))
}
function visualInflowMappedRecordMatches(record,filterState=visualInflowFilterState()){
  const options=filterState.options;
  if(!options.active)return!0;
  const rawRecords=visualInflowSourceRecords(record),categories=record?.categories||[];
  if(options.types.length){const direct=rawRecords.length?rawRecords.some(raw=>options.types.includes(searchFileType(raw))):options.types.some(type=>categories.includes(type)||"images"===type&&Boolean(record?.mediaUrl));if(!direct)return!1}
  if(!visualInflowFacetMatches(record,rawRecords,filterState.categories))return!1;
  if(!visualInflowFacetMatches(record,rawRecords,filterState.filters))return!1;
  if(!visualInflowFacetMatches(record,rawRecords,filterState.years))return!1;
  if(options.query&&!visualInflowFacetMatches(record,rawRecords,filterState.query,!0)&&!visualInflowTextMatches(record,options.query))return!1;
  return!0
}
function visualInflowScopeKey(options={}){const page="img"===state.mode?options.url?urlKey(options.url):"":urlKey(options.url||"");return[state.mode,page,visualSectionKey(options.section||""),options.worksOnly?1:0,options.ids?options.ids.size:""].join("|")}
async function visualInflowArchiveMedia(options={}){
  const scopeKey=visualInflowScopeKey(options),restore=state.search.visualArchiveRestore;
  if("img"===state.mode&&restore?.scopeKey===scopeKey){state.search.visualArchiveRestore=null;return restore.records.slice()}
  if(!visualInflowSearchActive())return visualInflowBaseRuntime.archiveMedia(options);
  const cache=state.search.visualArchiveCache;
  let source;
  if(cache?.scopeKey===scopeKey)source=cache.records.slice();
  else{const current=Array.isArray(state.archive.records)&&state.archive.records.length&&state.archive.key===scopeKey?state.archive.records.slice():null;source=current||await visualInflowBaseRuntime.archiveMedia(options),state.search.visualArchiveCache={scopeKey:scopeKey,records:source.slice()}}
  const filterState=visualInflowFilterState();
  return filterState.options.active?source.filter(record=>visualInflowMappedRecordMatches(record,filterState)):source
}
function visualInflowMoveActions(root,inFlow){
  const tools=document.getElementById("tools"),actions=root?.querySelector(".search-actions")||tools?.querySelector(':scope > .search-actions[data-afs-visual-actions="true"]'),form=root?.querySelector(".search-form"),divider=form?.querySelector(".search-form-divider");
  if(!actions||!form||!tools)return;
  if(inFlow){actions.dataset.afsVisualActions="true",actions.parentNode===tools||tools.append(actions)}
  else{actions.removeAttribute("data-afs-visual-actions"),actions.parentNode===form||form.insertBefore(actions,divider||null)}
}
function visualInflowInstallControlFocus(root){
  const actions=document.querySelector('#tools > .search-actions[data-afs-visual-actions="true"]');
  for(const surface of [root,actions]){if(!surface||"ready"===surface.dataset.afsInflowListeners)continue;surface.dataset.afsInflowListeners="ready";surface.addEventListener("click",event=>{if(!visualInflowSearchActive())return;const control=event.target.closest?.("button,[role=option]");control&&queueMicrotask(()=>{if(!visualInflowSearchActive())return;control.matches(".search-clear")?visualInflowTopField()?.focus({preventScroll:!0}):control.isConnected&&control.getClientRects().length&&control.focus({preventScroll:!0})})})}
}
function syncVisualInflowSearchShell(){
  const search=state.search,root=search.root;
  if(!root)return;
  const inFlow=visualInflowSearchActive(),form=root.querySelector(".search-form"),shade=root.querySelector(".search-shade"),status=root.querySelector(".search-status"),results=root.querySelector(".search-results");
  if(inFlow){
    deactivateSearchModal(),root.dataset.afsInflowSearch="true",root.setAttribute("role","search"),root.setAttribute("aria-label",state.mode==="card"?"Filter current cards":"Filter current images"),root.setAttribute("aria-modal","false"),root.removeAttribute("tabindex"),shade&&(shade.hidden=!0),search.systems&&(search.systems.hidden=!0),search.sectionNavigation&&(search.sectionNavigation.hidden=!0),status&&(status.hidden=!0),results&&(results.hidden=!0),form&&(form.hidden=!0),search.input&&(search.input.hidden=!0,search.input.tabIndex=-1,search.input.setAttribute("aria-hidden","true")),visualInflowMoveActions(root,!0),visualInflowInstallControlFocus(root);
    const archive=document.getElementById("archive"),grid=archive?.querySelector(":scope > .archive-grid");
    if(archive&&grid){
      let backing=document.getElementById("afs-img-search-backing");
      if("img"===state.mode){
        if(!backing){backing=document.createElement("div"),backing.id="afs-img-search-backing",backing.dataset.afsImgSearchBacking="true",backing.dataset.afsImgSearchBackingReady="false",backing.setAttribute("aria-hidden","true")}
        backing.parentNode===archive||archive.insertBefore(backing,grid),root.parentNode===archive&&root.nextSibling===grid||archive.insertBefore(root,grid);
        const syncBacking=()=>{
          if(!backing.isConnected||!root.isConnected)return void(backing.dataset.afsImgSearchBackingReady="false");
          const portals=[...document.querySelectorAll('body > .search-menu[data-search-menu-portal="true"]')],seen=backing._afsImgSearchObservedPortal,visible=node=>{if(!node)return!1;const style=getComputedStyle(node),bounds=node.getBoundingClientRect();return style.display!=="none"&&style.visibility!=="hidden"&&style.opacity!=="0"&&bounds.width>0&&bounds.height>0},nav=document.getElementById("W0675021862"),tools=document.getElementById("tools");
          if(!(Array.isArray(seen)&&seen.length===portals.length&&seen.every((node,index)=>node===portals[index]))){backing._afsImgSearchResizeObserver?.disconnect();const resizeObserver=new ResizeObserver(syncBacking);[nav,tools,root,...portals].filter(Boolean).forEach(node=>resizeObserver.observe(node)),backing._afsImgSearchResizeObserver=resizeObserver,backing._afsImgSearchPortalObserver?.disconnect();const portalObserver=new MutationObserver(syncBacking);portals.forEach(node=>portalObserver.observe(node,{attributes:!0,attributeFilter:["hidden","style","class","data-open"]})),backing._afsImgSearchPortalObserver=portalObserver,backing._afsImgSearchObservedPortal=portals}
          const bounds=[nav,tools,root,...portals].filter(visible).map(node=>node.getBoundingClientRect()),bottom=bounds.length?Math.max(...bounds.map(value=>value.bottom)):0,valid=bottom>0;
          valid?document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--afs-img-v1-ui-backing-bottom",(100*Math.ceil(bottom)/innerWidth)+"vw"):document.documentElement.style.removeProperty("--afs-img-v1-ui-backing-bottom"),backing.dataset.afsImgSearchBackingReady=valid?"true":"false",backing.dataset.afsImgUiBackingBottom=valid?String(Math.ceil(bottom)):"";
        };
        if(backing._afsImgSearchObservedRoot!==root){backing._afsImgSearchResizeObserver?.disconnect(),backing._afsImgSearchBodyObserver?.disconnect(),backing._afsImgSearchPortalObserver?.disconnect(),backing._afsImgSearchResizeHandler&&removeEventListener("resize",backing._afsImgSearchResizeHandler);const bodyObserver=new MutationObserver(syncBacking),resizeHandler=()=>syncBacking();bodyObserver.observe(document.body,{childList:!0}),addEventListener("resize",resizeHandler,{passive:!0}),backing._afsImgSearchBodyObserver=bodyObserver,backing._afsImgSearchResizeHandler=resizeHandler,backing._afsImgSearchObservedRoot=root,backing._afsImgSearchObservedPortal=void 0}
        requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(syncBacking))
      }else backing?._afsImgSearchResizeObserver?.disconnect(),backing?._afsImgSearchBodyObserver?.disconnect(),backing?._afsImgSearchPortalObserver?.disconnect(),backing?._afsImgSearchResizeHandler&&removeEventListener("resize",backing._afsImgSearchResizeHandler),document.documentElement.style.removeProperty("--afs-img-v1-ui-backing-bottom"),backing?.remove(),root.parentNode===archive&&root.nextSibling===grid||archive.insertBefore(root,grid)
    }
  }else{
    root.removeAttribute("data-afs-inflow-search"),root.setAttribute("role","dialog"),root.setAttribute("aria-label","Search"),root.setAttribute("aria-modal","true"),shade&&(shade.hidden=!1),search.systems&&(search.systems.hidden=!1),status&&(status.hidden=!1),results&&(results.hidden=!1),form&&(form.hidden=!1),search.input&&(search.input.hidden=!1,search.input.removeAttribute("tabindex"),search.input.removeAttribute("aria-hidden")),visualInflowMoveActions(root,!1),root.parentElement?.id==="archive"&&document.body.append(root),"true"===root.dataset.open&&activateSearchModal()
  }
}
function visualInflowAuthoredMatches(item,filterState){
  if(!filterState.options.active)return!0;
  const hashRecords=[];
  for(const media of item.querySelectorAll("media-item[hash]")){const hash=media.getAttribute("hash")||"";for(const record of visualInflowRecordMaps().byHash.get(hash)||[])hashRecords.push(record)}
  const route=urlKey(item.closest(".ppp-authored-deck")?.dataset.pppRoute||documentUrlKey()),mapped={id:"",hash:"",title:item.textContent||"",pageTitle:item.textContent||"",description:"",author:"",accessibleLabel:item.getAttribute("aria-label")||"",section:"P+",url:route,categories:[],mediaUrl:""};
  if(filterState.options.types.length&&!hashRecords.some(record=>filterState.options.types.includes(searchFileType(record))))return!1;
  if(!visualInflowFacetMatches(mapped,hashRecords,filterState.categories))return!1;
  if(!visualInflowFacetMatches(mapped,hashRecords,filterState.filters))return!1;
  if(!visualInflowFacetMatches(mapped,hashRecords,filterState.years))return!1;
  return!filterState.options.query||visualInflowFacetMatches(mapped,hashRecords,filterState.query,!0)||visualInflowTextMatches(mapped,filterState.options.query)
}
function finalizeVisualInflowArchive(){
  if(!visualInflowSearchActive())return;
  syncVisualInflowSearchShell();
  const archive=document.getElementById("archive"),grid=archive?.querySelector(":scope > .archive-grid"),status=archive?.querySelector(":scope > .archive-status");
  if(!archive||!grid)return;
  const filterState=visualInflowFilterState();
  let authored=0;
  for(const deck of grid.querySelectorAll(".ppp-authored-deck")){let deckCount=0;for(const item of deck.querySelectorAll(":scope > ol > li")){const visible=visualInflowAuthoredMatches(item,filterState);item.hidden=!visible,visible?item.style.removeProperty("display"):item.style.setProperty("display","none","important"),visible&&(deckCount+=1)}deck.hidden=!deckCount,deckCount?deck.style.removeProperty("display"):deck.style.setProperty("display","none","important"),authored+=deckCount}
  state.archive.authoredCount=authored;
  const generated=[...grid.querySelectorAll(":scope > .img-item,:scope > .card-item")].filter(item=>!item.hidden&&getComputedStyle(item).display!=="none").length,pending="true"===archive.getAttribute("aria-busy")?0:archiveUniqueRemaining(state.archive.rendered||0,new Set([...grid.querySelectorAll("[data-archive-id]")].map(node=>node.dataset.archiveId).filter(Boolean))),total=generated+pending+authored;
  if(filterState.options.active&&status){const noun="card"===state.mode?"card":"image",filteredStatus=`${total} matching ${noun}${1===total?"":"s"}${authored?` · ${authored} authored P+ card${1===authored?"":"s"}`:""}`;status.textContent!==filteredStatus&&(status.textContent=filteredStatus)}
  archive.dataset.afsFilterActive=String(filterState.options.active),archive.dataset.afsFilterQuery=filterState.options.query,archive.dataset.afsFilterTotal=String(total),archive.toggleAttribute("data-afs-filter-empty",filterState.options.active&&!total),state.search.root.dataset.afsFilterActive=String(filterState.options.active),state.search.root.dataset.afsFilterQuery=filterState.options.query
}
function observeVisualInflowArchive(){
  if(!visualInflowSearchActive())return stopVisualInflowArchiveObserver();
  const archive=document.getElementById("archive");
  if(!archive)return;
  if(state.search.visualArchiveObserved!==archive){state.search.visualArchiveObserver?.disconnect(),state.search.visualArchiveObserver=new MutationObserver(()=>queueMicrotask(finalizeVisualInflowArchive)),state.search.visualArchiveObserver.observe(archive,{childList:!0,subtree:!0}),state.search.visualArchiveObserved=archive}
  queueMicrotask(finalizeVisualInflowArchive),requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(finalizeVisualInflowArchive))
}
function stopVisualInflowArchiveObserver(){state.search.visualArchiveObserver?.disconnect(),state.search.visualArchiveObserver=null,state.search.visualArchiveObserved=null}
function resetVisualInflowSearch(rebuildImg=!1){
  const img="img"===state.mode,restore=img&&rebuildImg?state.search.visualArchiveCache:null;
  stopVisualInflowArchiveObserver(),state.search.visualArchiveRestore=restore,state.search.visualArchiveCache=null,state.search.visualArchiveFiltered=!1,state.search.visualFilterState=null;
  const root=state.search.root,archive=document.getElementById("archive"),backing=document.getElementById("afs-img-search-backing");
  backing?._afsImgSearchResizeObserver?.disconnect(),backing?._afsImgSearchBodyObserver?.disconnect(),backing?._afsImgSearchPortalObserver?.disconnect(),backing?._afsImgSearchResizeHandler&&removeEventListener("resize",backing._afsImgSearchResizeHandler),document.documentElement.style.removeProperty("--afs-img-v1-ui-backing-bottom"),backing?.remove();
  root&&(root.removeAttribute("data-afs-inflow-search"),root.parentElement?.id==="archive"&&document.body.append(root)),archive&&(archive.removeAttribute("data-afs-filter-active"),archive.removeAttribute("data-afs-filter-query"),archive.removeAttribute("data-afs-filter-total"),archive.removeAttribute("data-afs-filter-empty"));
  syncVisualInflowSearchShell();
  if(img&&!rebuildImg)return;
  visualInflowBaseRuntime.syncArchive(!0)
}
openSearch=function(query="",trigger,options={}){
  const topField=trigger?.matches?.("#tools .search-field"),selectionOptions=["section","category","kind","filetype","type","filter","year"].some(name=>Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(options,name));
  if(visualInflowSearchActive()&&topField&&!selectionOptions){const search=state.search;search.input.value=String(query??"").replace(/^SEARCH:\s*/i,""),search.root.dataset.typed=String(Boolean(search.input.value.trim())),syncSearchField(),clearTimeout(search.inputTimer),search.inputTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(state.destroyed||!visualInflowSearchActive())return;search.inputTimer=0,state.search.visualFilterState=null,updateSearchUrl(!0),renderSearch()},90),syncVisualInflowSearchShell(),!1!==options.focus&&trigger.focus({preventScroll:!0});return}
  const result=visualInflowBaseRuntime.openSearch(query,trigger,options);
  if(visualInflowSearchActive()){state.search.section="",state.search.navigationSection="",state.search.visualFilterState=null,syncVisualInflowSearchShell(),renderSearchFilters(),renderSearch(),!1!==options.history&&updateSearchUrl(!0);if(!1!==options.focus){const field=visualInflowTopField();field?.focus({preventScroll:!0}),field?.setSelectionRange?.(field.value.length,field.value.length)}}
  return result
};
closeSearch=function(fromHistory=!1){const wasVisual=visualInflowSearchActive(),img=wasVisual&&"img"===state.mode,rebuildImg=img&&(state.search.visualArchiveFiltered||visualInflowOptions().active),archive=img?document.getElementById("archive"):null,archiveTop=archive?.scrollTop||0,pageX=img?scrollX:0,pageY=img?scrollY:0,previous=state.search.previous,returnTo=img&&!fromHistory&&previous?.isConnected&&!previous.closest?.("#search")?previous:null;wasVisual&&state.search.input&&(state.search.input.value=""),img&&(state.search.previous=null);const result=visualInflowBaseRuntime.closeSearch(img?!0:fromHistory);if(wasVisual&&resetVisualInflowSearch(rebuildImg),img){const restore=()=>{scrollTo(pageX,pageY),archive&&(archive.scrollTop=archiveTop)};restore(),requestAnimationFrame(()=>{restore(),requestAnimationFrame(restore)}),returnTo&&returnTo.isConnected&&!matchMedia("(hover: none), (pointer: coarse)").matches&&(state.returningFocus=!0,returnTo.focus({preventScroll:!0}),queueMicrotask(()=>{state.returningFocus=!1}),restore())}return result};
prepareSearchNavigation=function(){const wasVisual=visualInflowSearchActive(),rebuildImg=wasVisual&&"img"===state.mode&&(state.search.visualArchiveFiltered||visualInflowOptions().active),result=visualInflowBaseRuntime.prepareSearchNavigation();return wasVisual&&resetVisualInflowSearch(rebuildImg),result};
renderSearchFilters=function(){
  const inFlow=visualInflowSearchActive();
  inFlow&&(state.search.section="",state.search.navigationSection="");
  const result=visualInflowBaseRuntime.renderSearchFilters();
  return inFlow&&(state.search.systems?.replaceChildren(),state.search.systems&&(state.search.systems.hidden=!0),state.search.sectionNavigation?.replaceChildren(),state.search.sectionNavigation&&(state.search.sectionNavigation.hidden=!0),syncVisualInflowSearchShell()),result
};
renderSearch=function(){
  if(!visualInflowSearchActive())return syncVisualInflowSearchShell(),visualInflowBaseRuntime.renderSearch();
  const search=state.search,query=search.input?.value||"",options=visualInflowOptions();
  search.observer?.disconnect(),search.observer=null,releaseCardCaptionObservers(search.list),search.list?.replaceChildren(),search.results=[],search.rendered=0,search.groupSerial=0,search.root.dataset.typed=String(Boolean(query.trim())),search.root.dataset.state=options.active?"filtering":"empty",search.root.dataset.afsResultState="in-flow",search.root.removeAttribute("data-afs-result-total"),search.root.removeAttribute("data-afs-result-loaded"),search.statusText&&(search.statusText.textContent=""),"loading"===state.dataStatus?search.root.setAttribute("aria-busy","true"):search.root.removeAttribute("aria-busy"),state.search.visualFilterState=null,syncVisualInflowSearchShell();
  if("img"===state.mode&&!options.active){const cache=state.search.visualArchiveCache;if(state.search.visualArchiveFiltered&&cache){state.search.visualArchiveFiltered=!1,state.search.visualArchiveRestore=cache;return syncArchive(!0)}return void(state.search.visualArchiveFiltered=!1)}
  "img"===state.mode&&(state.search.visualArchiveFiltered=!0),syncArchive(!0)
};
searchKeys=function(event){
  if(!visualInflowSearchActive())return visualInflowBaseRuntime.searchKeys(event);
  if(event.defaultPrevented||searchModalQuickViewActive())return;
  if("Escape"===event.key){const open=state.search.root.querySelector('.search-select[data-open="true"]');return open?closeSelect(open,!0):closeSearch(),event.preventDefault(),void event.stopPropagation()}
  if("ArrowDown"===event.key&&(document.activeElement===visualInflowTopField()||document.activeElement===state.search.input)){const first=state.search.root.querySelector('.search-categories button,.search-filetypes button,.search-advanced button');first&&(event.preventDefault(),first.focus({preventScroll:!0}))}
};
searchFocusGuard=function(event){return visualInflowSearchActive()?void 0:visualInflowBaseRuntime.searchFocusGuard(event)};
archiveMedia=visualInflowArchiveMedia;
syncArchive=function(force=!1){
  const root=state.search.root;
  !visualInflowMode()&&root?.parentElement?.id==="archive"&&document.body.append(root);
  const result=visualInflowBaseRuntime.syncArchive(force);
  return visualInflowSearchActive()?(syncVisualInflowSearchShell(),observeVisualInflowArchive()):stopVisualInflowArchiveObserver(),result
};

function footerHtmlProjectedDocument() {
  const root = document.documentElement;
  return root.dataset.afsMode === "html"
    && root.dataset.afsHtmlV1Projected === "preparse"
    && root.dataset.afsHtmlV1Preparse === "ready"
    && !nativePreviewAllowed();
}
function footerHtmlV1OwnedDocument() {
  const root = document.documentElement;
  return root.dataset.afsMode === "html"
    && (footerHtmlProjectedDocument() || nativePreviewAllowed());
}
function footerHtmlNativeV1Lifecycle() {
  return document.documentElement.dataset.afsMode === "html"
    && nativePreviewAllowed()
    && globalThis.AFSHtmlModeV1?.nativeLifecycle === true;
}
function footerHtmlIconIsProjected(icon) {
  return footerHtmlV1OwnedDocument()
    && icon.hasAttribute("data-afs-html-v1-media");
}
function footerHtmlIconAttribute(icon, name) {
  return icon.getAttribute(footerHtmlIconIsProjected(icon)
    ? `data-afs-html-v1-${name}` : name);
}
function footerHtmlCanonicalizeIconSource(icon) {
  const source = footerHtmlIconAttribute(icon, "contenturl");
  if (!/^https:\/\/mirrors\.creativecommons\.org\/presskit\/icons\/(?:cc|by|nc|nd)\.svg\?ref=chooser-v1$/.test(source || "")) {
    throw new Error("footer icon source identity mismatch");
  }
  if (footerHtmlIconIsProjected(icon)) {
    icon.setAttribute("data-afs-html-v1-src", source);
    icon.removeAttribute("src");
  } else {
    icon.setAttribute("src", source);
  }
}
function footerEmptyAccepted(topology, contract, semantic, matches) {
  const exactSemantic = "94f0904976d25104f8314ce85b9aa0931d8f269c0a9b63ea2347eaf9fb3c848a";
  const noSignificantChildren = [...topology.gallery.childNodes].every((node) =>
    node.nodeType === 8 || (node.nodeType === 3 && !String(node.textContent || "").trim()));
  return top === window
    && !/^\/edit(?:\/|$)/.test(location.pathname)
    && contract.entries === 92
    && contract.semanticSha256 === exactSemantic
    && semantic === exactSemantic
    && topology.items.length === 0
    && !topology.gallery.querySelector("media-item")
    && noSignificantChildren
    && matches(topology, contract, "live");
}

const decodeShard=async node=>{
 const binary=atob(node.textContent.replace(/\s+/g,"")),bytes=Uint8Array.from(binary,character=>character.charCodeAt(0));

const buildRecordIndex=async()=>{
 let nodes=[];
 for(let attempt=0;attempt<1200;attempt++){
  nodes=[document.getElementById("afs-site-data-shard-1"),document.getElementById("afs-site-data-shard-2")];
  if(nodes.every(Boolean))break;
  await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,25));
 }
 if(!nodes.every(Boolean))return new Map;
 const parts=[];
 for(const node of nodes){parts.push(await decodeShard(node));await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,0))}
 parts.sort((a,b)=>a.i-b.i);
 const columns=parts[0]?.c||[],byRoute=new Map;
 for(const row of parts.flatMap(part=>part.r||[])){
  const record=Object.fromEntries(columns.map((column,index)=>[column,row[index]])),route=routeKey(record.u||"/");
  if(!byRoute.has(route))byRoute.set(route,[]);
  byRoute.get(route).push(record);
 }
 return byRoute;
};

const fileKind=record=>{
 const recordType=record.g||"";
 if(!["media","document","video","book"].includes(recordType))return"";
 const categories=Array.isArray(record.c)?record.c:[],url=mediaUrl(record);

 if(categories.includes("installation-views"))return"installation-views";

 if(recordType==="video"||categories.includes("videos")||/\.(?:mp4|m4v|mov|webm)(?:$|[?#])/i.test(url))return"videos";
 if(recordType==="book"||categories.includes("images")||record.th)return"images";
 return"";
};

  media.setAttribute("alt",label||record.t||"Media");
  media.setAttribute("aria-hidden","true");
  media.tabIndex=-1;
  if(hash)media.setAttribute("hash",hash);
  button.append(media);
 }
 button.addEventListener("click",event=>{event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();quickView(record)});
 item.append(button);
 return item;
};
const relatedDisclosure=(kind,records)=>{
 const labels=relatedLabels[kind],details=make("details","search-related search-related-group"),summary=make("summary","search-count"),list=make("ol","search-related-list");
 details.dataset.fileType=kind;
 const closed=()=>`+ ${records.length} ${records.length===1?labels.one:labels.many}`;
 const sync=()=>setText(summary,details.open?`− ${labels.open}`:closed());
 summary.setAttribute("aria-label",closed());
 records.forEach(record=>list.append(relatedItem(record)));
 details.addEventListener("toggle",sync);
 details.append(summary,list);
 sync();
 return details;
};
const audioDisclosure=(records,route)=>{
 const tracks=records.filter(record=>(record.g||"")==="audio"||(Array.isArray(record.c)&&record.c.includes("audio"))),seen=new Set,unique=[];
 for(const track of tracks){const key=`${track.a||""}|${track.t||""}`;if(!seen.has(key)){seen.add(key);unique.push(track)}}
 if(!unique.length)return null;
 const details=make("details","search-related search-related-group search-related-audio"),summary=make("summary","search-count"),list=make("ol","search-album-tracks"),platformUrl=audioDestinations.get(route)||"";
 details.dataset.fileType="audio";
 const closed=()=>`+ ${unique.length} Audio`;
 const sync=()=>setText(summary,details.open?"− Audio":closed());
 summary.setAttribute("aria-label",closed());
 for(const track of unique){
  const item=make("li","search-album-track"),link=make("a","search-track-link"),author=make("span","search-track-author",track.a||""),title=make("i","",track.t||"Untitled"),href=/^https?:\/\//i.test(track.q||"")?track.q:platformUrl||route,external=/^https?:\/\//i.test(href);
  link.href=href;
  if(external){link.target="_blank";link.rel="noopener noreferrer"}
  else link.rel="history";
  link.setAttribute("aria-label",[track.a,track.t].filter(Boolean).join(", "));
  if(track.a)link.append(author,document.createTextNode(", "));
  link.append(title);
  item.append(link);list.append(item);
 }
 details.addEventListener("toggle",sync);
 details.append(summary,list);
 sync();
 return details;
};
const refineProjectItem=item=>{
 if((item.dataset.resultGroup||"")!=="projects")return;
 const link=item.querySelector(".search-link[href]");
 if(!link)return;
 const route=item.dataset.afsProjectRoute||routeKey(link.getAttribute("href")||"");
 if(!item.dataset.afsProjectRoute)item.dataset.afsProjectRoute=route;
 const presentation=projectPresentations.get(route);
 if(!presentation)return;
 const detail=item.querySelector(".search-detail");
 if(presentation.date&&detail)setText(detail,presentation.date);
 if(presentation.destination){
  const source=item.querySelector(".search-source:not(.search-media-source) .search-url");
  for(const destinationLink of [link,source].filter(Boolean)){
   destinationLink.href=presentation.destination;
   destinationLink.target="_blank";
   destinationLink.rel="noopener noreferrer";
  }
  if(source)setText(source,presentation.destination);
 }
};
const refineRelatedItem=item=>{
 if(!recordIndex)return;
 const group=item.dataset.resultGroup||"",link=item.querySelector(".search-link[href]"),body=item.querySelector(".search-body"),route=item.dataset.afsProjectRoute||routeKey(link?.getAttribute("href")||"");
 if(!body||!route||route==="/")return;
 const signature=`${group}|${route}`;
 if(item.dataset.afsRelatedSignature===signature)return;
 item.querySelectorAll(":scope .search-related,:scope .search-album-tracks").forEach(node=>node.remove());
 const routeRecords=recordIndex.get(route)||[],bookTitle=(item.querySelector(".search-title i")?.textContent||"").trim().toLocaleLowerCase(),records=group==="books"&&["/screen","/m","/before-arts"].includes(route)?routeRecords.filter(record=>(record.t||"").trim().toLocaleLowerCase()===bookTitle):routeRecords;
 if(group==="albums"||group==="pages"){
  const audio=audioDisclosure(records,route);
  if(audio)body.append(audio);
 }
 if(eligibleRelatedGroups.has(group)){
  const primary=recordHash({q:item.querySelector(".search-thumb")?.getAttribute("contenturl")||"",th:item.querySelector(".search-thumb")?.getAttribute("src")||""}),seen=new Set,grouped=new Map(relatedOrder.map(kind=>[kind,[]]));
  for(const record of records){
   const kind=fileKind(record),url=mediaUrl(record),hash=recordHash(record),key=hash||url;
   if(!kind||!relatedOrder.includes(kind)||!url||hash&&hash===primary||seen.has(key))continue;
   seen.add(key);grouped.get(kind).push(record);
  }
  for(const kind of relatedOrder){const related=grouped.get(kind);if(related.length)body.append(relatedDisclosure(kind,related))}
 }
 item.dataset.afsRelatedSignature=signature;
};

const refine=()=>{
 frame=0;
 if(html.hasAttribute("data-mode"))return;
 const search=document.getElementById("search");
 if(!search)return;
 restructureNavigation(search);
 enhanceMoreButton(search);
 syncThumbnailMeasure(search);
 search.querySelectorAll(".search-item").forEach(refineProjectItem);
 html.dataset.afsSearchRelatedIndex="native-lazy";
};
const queue=()=>{if(!frame)frame=requestAnimationFrame(refine)};
new MutationObserver(queue).observe(html,{childList:true,subtree:true});
addEventListener("resize",queue,{passive:true});
document.addEventListener("afs:search-sentinel",queue);
queue();
})();})()
;

(()=>{(()=>{"use strict";
const root=document.documentElement;
let queued=false;
const blocked="script,style,noscript,template,svg,media-item,picture,img,video,audio,input,textarea,.afs-pre-natal-invert-glyph,.afs-pre-natal-search-value";
function decorateScope(scope){
  const walker=document.createTreeWalker(scope,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT,{acceptNode(node){
    const parent=node.parentElement;
    return parent&&node.nodeValue.trim()&&!parent.closest(blocked)?NodeFilter.FILTER_ACCEPT:NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT;
  }});
  const nodes=[];
  while(walker.nextNode())nodes.push(walker.currentNode);
  for(const node of nodes){
    const span=document.createElement("span");
    span.className="afs-pre-natal-invert-glyph";
    node.replaceWith(span);
    span.append(node);
    const parent=span.parentElement;
    if(parent&&parent.matches("button,[role='option'],.search-clear,.search-close,.search-system,.search-category,.search-filetype,.search-type,.search-trigger,.search-option,.search-more")){
      parent.classList.add("afs-pre-natal-invert-surface");
    }
  }
}
function decorate(){
  if(root.dataset.system!=="pre-natal"||(root.dataset.afsMode||root.dataset.mode))return;
  const box=document.querySelector("#search .search-box");
  if(box){
    decorateScope(box);
    const input=box.querySelector(".search-input"),form=input?.closest(".search-form");
    if(input&&form){
      form.classList.add("afs-pre-natal-invert-surface");
      let mirror=form.querySelector(":scope > .afs-pre-natal-search-value");
      if(!mirror){
        mirror=document.createElement("span");
        mirror.className="afs-pre-natal-search-value";
        mirror.setAttribute("aria-hidden","true");
        input.insertAdjacentElement("afterend",mirror);
      }
      const value=input.value||"";
      if(mirror.textContent!==value)mirror.textContent=value;
    }
  }
  for(const portal of document.querySelectorAll('body > .search-menu[data-search-menu-portal="true"]'))decorateScope(portal);
}
function schedule(){
  if(queued)return;
  queued=true;
  queueMicrotask(()=>{queued=false;decorate()});
}

})();})()

  if(mine===t&&t.style.paddingBottom!==val){mine=null;val="";return}
  if(!apply){timer=setTimeout(()=>check(!0),700);return}
  const k=(tries.get(t)||0)+1;tries.set(t,k);if(k>5){dead.add(t);return}
  const v=(h/(Math.min(innerWidth,innerHeight)/100)).toFixed(4)+"vmin";
  t.style.paddingBottom=v;t.style.setProperty("--pin-padding-bottom",v);t.dataset.afsPinPad="1";mine=t;val=t.style.paddingBottom;
  Math.abs((parseFloat(getComputedStyle(t).paddingBottom)||0)-h)>1&&(release(),dead.add(t))}

})();}catch(error){}
